- **communicate-consulting-message**: Frame and deliver one or a few concise consulting messages from a central-case-lead-approved answer. Use for recommendation, diagnosis, insight, status, risk, options, learning, or operating-instruction messages in meetings, emails, updates, and slide titles. Consume the existing Audience/Onboarding Contract, preserve the approved claim state and evidence boundary, and choose a family-appropriate close. Do not decompose the problem, adjudicate hypotheses, change the overall answer, sequence a full storyline, or design slides. - **communicating-agents**: Agent-to-agent communication protocols for multi-agent interoperability: A2A (Agent2Agent) -- Agent Cards, task-based messaging, discovery, streaming, push notifications, Python/TypeScript SDKs, ADK/LangGraph/CrewAI/Pydantic AI integration. Triggers: building multi-agent systems across frameworks or organizations, exposing agents via A2A endpoints, implementing agent discovery; A2A, agent-to-agent, Agent Card, multi-agent communication, agent interoperability, cross-agent protocol. Python/TypeScript modules. ([francisco-perez-sorrosal/praxion](https://github.com/francisco-perez-sorrosal/praxion/tree/HEAD/skills/communicating-agents) · ★2)
- **communicating-clearly**: Use when a conversational response is running long, padded, hedged, or unclear — or when explicitly asked to tighten, shorten, simplify, or "get to the point" mid-conversation. For chat replies, not authored documents (see drafting-for-readers for those). ([introvenk/clear-business-writing-skills](https://github.com/introvenk/clear-business-writing-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/communicating-clearly) · ★1)
- **communicating-clearly**: Use when composing any reply that a person will read in conversation — status updates, progress reports, plan summaries, explanations, findings, review verdicts, or answers to questions — especially in long sessions where shorthand, codenames, or compressed phrasing have built up ([mnbf9rca/claude-plugins](https://github.com/mnbf9rca/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/writing-tools/skills/communicating-clearly))
- **communicating-concisely**: Use when the user asks for caveman mode, fewer tokens, brief responses, compressed communication, or otherwise explicitly requests a much shorter answer. ([GanyuanRan/Aegis](https://github.com/GanyuanRan/Aegis/tree/HEAD/skills/communicating-concisely) · ★1.2k)
- **communicating-decisions**: Communicate product decisions so teams understand not just what was decided but why. Covers framing, transparency about trade-offs, and handling disagreement gracefully. Use when decisions aren't sticking, teams are relitigating past calls, or when stakeholders feel left out. ([Dragoon0x/product-skills](https://github.com/Dragoon0x/product-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/decision-making/communicating-decisions) · ★4)
- **communicating-in-html**: When loaded: deliver reports, intake interviews (&gt;3 fields), and option/mockup choices as one self-contained offline .html page instead of chat Markdown. Forms return answers via a copy-paste token. ([kzarzycki/agent-skills](https://github.com/kzarzycki/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/experimental/skills/communicating-in-html))
- **communicating-in-mdx**: Optional layer: write specs, plans, designs, and reports as MDX (Markdown + a component library) rendered locally via Vite — git-diffable, degrades to plain Markdown, no remote services. ([kzarzycki/agent-skills](https://github.com/kzarzycki/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/experimental/skills/communicating-in-mdx))
